What’s happening today

  - Aux pairs still call calculate_overdue_pairs() with only draws[:100] (see src/app.py:1414). That means repeating-
  pair thresholds at 107+ can never be reached—so a pair that’s truly “red” across the full history is being clipped to
  the last 100 draws.
  - Every other overdue/check we rely on either uses the whole draw history or at least a much deeper window (e.g.,
  combo draws-since leverages 1 000 draws; the doubles history fetch uses full draws per state). So pairs are the
  outlier.
  - Due Doubles: already grounded in Combined top-4 pairs and the combo cache sweeps C/M/E variants. We keep the top
  four from Combined only and gather variant combos with draws-since ≥ 1 000. That wiring looks sound.

  Brainstorming safe adjustments (no code yet)

  1. Window depth – Instead of hard-capping at 100, we can lift the window to something that easily covers the RED/BLUE
  thresholds but still keeps compute manageable.
      - We can make the window size configurable (module constant) so future tweaks are easy.
  2. Alignment with other features – Candidates/combos already treat permutations as boxed, so using the same window for
  pairs keeps the mental model consistent (“scanning most-current X draws boxed”). We’d also note that boxes hit more
  frequently than straight singles, so 350 draws is plenty to catch reds but not so high that we degrade responsiveness.
  3. Integrity checks – Before changing, I’ll run two validations:
      - quick script to compare the current 100-draw calculation against a 350-draw pass on a couple of states to
  confirm we now see reds while blues stay aligned.
      - ensure the Overdue Pairs table (Aux) and Control Center Due Doubles still show coherent numbers (no regressions
  around the top-5 block).
  4. Docs/log – Tiny note in the changelog & Aux docs (Pairs thresholds section) to record we now scan 350 draws.

  If that all sounds reasonable, I’ll scope the actual change to:

  - add a PAIRS_ANALYSIS_WINDOW = 350 constant in calculate_overdue_pairs() (or the caller), use
  draws[:PAIRS_ANALYSIS_WINDOW];
  - leave everything else untouched;
  - re-run py_compile and spot-check in the UI.

  Let me know if 350 feels right or if you’d rather I swing to another window length (e.g., 300 or 500). Once you’re
  good with the plan I’ll wire it in.

    import textwrap
    path = Path(r'scripts/auxiliary/working/modules/analyze_pairs.py')
    text = path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')
    start = text.index('def calculate_overdue_pairs')

    new_func = '''def calculate_overdue_pairs(draws: List[str], window: int = PAIRS_ANALYSIS_WINDOW) -> Tuple[Dict[str,
        int], Dict[str, int], Dict[str, str]]:
        \"\"\"Calculate draws-since status for every canonical pair within a recent window.\"\"\"
        if draws is None:
            return {}, {}, {}

        if window is None or window <= 0:
            analysis_draws = list(draws)
        else:
            analysis_draws = draws[:window]

        total = len(analysis_draws)
        if total == 0:
            return {}, {}, {}

        last_seen: Dict[str, int] = {}
        times_drawn: Dict[str, int] = {}

        for i, draw in enumerate(analysis_draws):
            if not isinstance(draw, str) or len(draw) "'!= 3:
                continue

            d1, d2, d3 = draw[0], draw[1], draw[2]
            raw_pairs = [d1 + d2, d2 + d3, d1 + d3]

            for raw_pair in raw_pairs:
                pair = '"''.join(sorted(raw_pair))
                times_drawn[pair] = times_drawn.get(pair, 0) + 1
                if pair not in last_seen:
                    last_seen[pair] = i

        non_repeating_overdue: Dict[str, int] = {}
        repeating_overdue: Dict[str, int] = {}
        pair_status: Dict[str, str] = {}

        red_count = 0
        blue_count = 0
        purple_count = 0

        for p in range(10):
            for q in range(p, 10):
                pair = f\"{p}{q}\"
                overdue_count = last_seen.get(pair, total)
                times_seen = times_drawn.get(pair, 0)
                is_repeating = pair[0] == pair[1]

                if is_repeating:
                    repeating_overdue[pair] = overdue_count

                    if overdue_count >= THRESHOLD_VERY_LATE_REPEATING:
                        pair_status[pair] = COLOR_LATE
                        red_count += 1
                        print(f\"DEBUG: Repeating pair {pair} is RED (>= {THRESHOLD_VERY_LATE_REPEATING}) with
        {overdue_count} draws overdue, seen {times_seen} times\")
                    elif overdue_count >= THRESHOLD_LATE_REPEATING:
                        pair_status[pair] = COLOR_VERY_LATE
                        blue_count += 1
                        print(f\"DEBUG: Repeating pair {pair} is BLUE (>= {THRESHOLD_LATE_REPEATING}) with
        {overdue_count} draws overdue, seen {times_seen} times\")
                    elif overdue_count >= THRESHOLD_PENDING_LATE:
                        pair_status[pair] = COLOR_PENDING
                        purple_count += 1
                        print(f\"DEBUG: Repeating pair {pair} is PURPLE (pending) with {overdue_count} draws overdue,
        seen {times_seen} times\")
                else:
                    non_repeating_overdue[pair] = overdue_count

                        print(f\"DEBUG: Non-repeating pair {pair} is PURPLE (pending) with {overdue_count} draws
        overdue, seen {times_seen} times\")

        print(f\"DEBUG: Found {red_count} RED pairs, {blue_count} BLUE pairs, and {purple_count} PURPLE pairs\")

        return non_repeating_overdue, repeating_overdue, pair_status
    '''
    new_text = text[:start] + new_func + text[end+1:]
    path.write_text(new_text, encoding='utf-8')
    "

  What’s in place today

  gives us the compound features you care about.
  - In src/app.py the Aux page currently renders those results as three separate tabs (Combined/Midday/Evening) with
  simple rank/score tables per position. There’s no visual tracker like your markdown mock-up, and the overall Aux
  layout still has each feature in variant-by-variant stacks rather than the unified, side-by-side grid you described.
  - Control Center is already consuming the positional “heat” strings for Due Doubles, so the data contract is reliable;
  we just need a richer UI + more explicit feature surfaces.

  Tracker design (from position_research + task_31)

  - One consolidated panel per variant, each with a 3×3 grid (ranks × positions) showing Draws Since and the highlighted
  digit. Hard-due digits (≥55 Combined, ≥40 Midday/Evening) turn the digit red inside the yellow cell.
  - The same grid should double as a scoreboard feed: cross-variant confirmation, mirror echoes, double-pressure flags,
  etc., become badges/notes alongside the grid so you can compound features later.
  - Downstream candidates (top straight/boxed suggestions) keep using the existing PPA scoring, but we can expose them
  right under the grid with clear tags (“P1#1”, “Mirror”, “VTRAC”, “DueSum”, etc.).

  Aux layout changes you requested

  - Remove the three separate “variant tabs” for every feature. Instead, present a single, big Aux view where each
  feature shows Midday/Evening/Combined side by side:
      - Top row: V‑TRAC boxed tables — three columns, one per variant.
      - Subsequent rows: tracking tables (pairs, index hits, sums, positional tracker, combo analysis, BA alerts, etc.)
  aligned so you can scan across variants quickly.
      - The existing summary strings (positional heat, consensus notes) can live as captions beneath each variant column
  so they don’t clutter the grids.
  - Keep the existing data loaders/caching; we’re only changing presentation and adding the tracker output.

        \n            if fresh_tag:\\n                entry.add_component(\"recent_brake\", -0.2 * base, fresh_tag)\\n
        else:\\n                entry.add_component(\"lag\", 0.1 * base)\\n\\n        digits_in_position = {e.digit: e
        for e in top_raw}\\n        for entry in top_raw:\\n            mirror_digit = MIRROR_MAP.get(entry.digit)\\n
        if mirror_digit is None:\\n                continue\\n            friend = digits_in_position.get(mirror_digit)\
        \n            if friend is None:\\n                continue\\n            bonus = cfg.mirror_same_variant
        * friend.score_components.get(\"base\", 0.0)\\n            entry.add_component(\"mirror_local\",
        bonus, cfg.mirror_tag)\\n\\n        summaries[pos] = PositionSummary(\\n            position=pos,
        top_digits=top_raw, population=population, window=window\\n        )\\n\\n    return VariantPositionalResult(\\n
        variant=variant, window=window, draws_used=len(clean), position_summaries=summaries\\n    )\\n\\n'''

    if needle not in text:
        raise SystemExit('analyze_variant block not found')

    replacement = \"    tracker_grid: Dict[PositionIndex, List[PositionalTrackerCell]] = {}\\n\" + needle.replace(
        \"summaries[pos] = PositionSummary(\\n            position=pos, top_digits=top_raw, population=population,
        window=window\\n        )\\n\\n    return VariantPositionalResult(\\n        variant=variant, window=window,
        draws_used=len(clean), position_summaries=summaries\\n    )\\n\\n\",
        \"        tracker_grid[pos] = [\\n            PositionalTrackerCell(\\n                digit=entry.digit,\
        \n                draws_since=entry.gap,\\n                hard_due=entry.gap >=
HARD_DUE_THRESHOLDS.get(variant,
        55),\\n            )\\n            for entry in top_raw\\n        ]\\n        summaries[pos] = PositionSummary(\
        \n            position=pos, top_digits=top_raw, population=population, window=window\\n        )\\n\\n    return
        VariantPositionalResult(\\n        variant=variant,\\n        window=window,\\n        draws_used=len(clean),\\n
        position_summaries=summaries,\\n        tracker_grid=tracker_grid,\\n    )\\n\\n\"
    )
    text = text.replace(needle, replacement)

    # Update analyze_variant top-level import for Dict? Already present.
    # ensure __all__ includes PositionalTrackerCell maybe? We'll add.
    if \"PositionalTrackerCell\" not in text:
        raise SystemExit('PositionalTrackerCell not inserted?')

    from textwrap import dedent

    new_block = dedent(\"\"\"
    variant_cache: Dict[str, Optional[dict]] = {}

                    def _render_tracker_table(title, tracker_grid, draws_used, source_path):
                        tracker_header = \"<thead><tr><th>Rank</th><th>P1 DS</th><th>P1 DIG</th><th>P2 DS</th><th>P2
        DIG</th><th>P3 DS</th><th>P3 DIG</th></tr></thead>\"
                        body_rows = []
                        for rank in range(3):
                            row_cells = [f\"<td>{rank + 1}</td>\"]
                            for pos_idx in range(3):
                                cells = tracker_grid.get(pos_idx, [])
                                if rank < len(cells):
                                    cell = cells[rank]
                                    ds_html = cell.draws_since
                                    digit_style = \"background-color:#ffea70;padding:0 6px;\"
                                    if cell.hard_due:
                                        digit_style += \"color:#d60000;font-weight:600;\"
                                    digit_html = f\"<span style='{digit_style}'>{cell.digit}</span>\"
                                else:
                                    ds_html = \"&mdash;\"
                                    digit_html = \"<span style='background-color:#ffea70;padding:0 6px;'>&mdash;</
        span>\"
                                row_cells.append(f\"<td>{ds_html}</td>\")
                                row_cells.append(f\"<td>{digit_html}</td>\")
                            body_rows.append(\"<tr>\" + \"\".join(row_cells) + \"</tr>\")
                        table_html = (
                            \"<table class='pos-tracker'>\"
                            + tracker_header
                            + \"<tbody>\"
                            + \"\".join(body_rows)
                            + \"</tbody></table>\"
                        )
                        st.markdown(table_html, unsafe_allow_html=True)
                        meta_bits: List[str] = []
                        if source_path:
                            try:
                                meta_bits.append(f\"Draw source: {_Path(source_path).name}\")
                            except Exception:
                                meta_bits.append(f\"Draw source: {source_path}\")
                        if draws_used:
                            meta_bits.append(f\"Draws used: {draws_used}\")
                        if meta_bits:
                            st.caption(\" | \".join(meta_bits))

                    def _render_vtrac_box(payload):
                        data = payload.get(\"data\", {})
                        vstat = data.get(\"vstat\") or {}
                        if not vstat:
                            st.caption(\"No V-TRAC data\")
                            return
                        pair_status = data.get(\"pair_status\") or {}
                        draws = data.get(\"draws\") or []
                        draws_1000 = data.get(\"draws_1000\") or draws
                        sums_stats = data.get(\"sums_stats\") or {}
                        index_first_seen = {}
                        total_len = len(draws_1000)
                        try:
                            for idx, draw_value in enumerate(draws_1000):
                                if not isinstance(draw_value, str) or len(draw_value) "'!= 3 or len(set(draw_value))
        == 1:
                                    continue
                                idx_tmp = get_vtrac_index(draw_value)
                                if idx_tmp and idx_tmp not in index_first_seen:
                                    index_first_seen[idx_tmp] = idx
                        except Exception:
                            index_first_seen = {}
                        index_draws_since_overlay = {i: index_first_seen.get(i, total_len) for i in range(1, 36)}
                        _sorted_by_ds_overlay = sorted(index_draws_since_overlay.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1],
        reverse=True)
                        _top10_overdue_overlay = [i for i, _ds in _sorted_by_ds_overlay[:10]]
                        rows = []
                        for entry in VTRAC_DISPLAY:
                            idx = entry["Index"]
                            singles = entry["Singles"].split() if entry.get("Singles") else []
                            doubles = entry["Doubles"].split() if entry.get("Doubles") else []
                            sdict = vstat.get(idx, {}).get("singles_status", {})
                            ddict = vstat.get(idx, {}).get("doubles_status", {})
                            s_html = " ".join([
                                (_format_combo(combo, sdict, pair_status) + _sums_badge_for(combo, sums_stats))
                                for combo in singles
                            ]) if singles else "&nbsp;"
                            d_html = " ".join([
                                (_format_combo(combo, ddict, pair_status) + _sums_badge_for(combo, sums_stats))
                                for combo in doubles
                            ]) if doubles else "&nbsp;"
                            idx_style = vstat.get(idx, {}).get("index_style", {})
                            row_class = ""
                            badge_html = ""
                            if idx_style.get("bg") == "green":
                                row_class = "row-green"
                                rank_value = idx_style.get("rank")
                                badge_html = f'"'<sup class=\"rank-badge\">{rank_value}</sup>' if rank_value else \"\"
                            else:
                                if idx in _top10_overdue_overlay:
                                    row_class = \"row-red\"
                                    rank_num = _top10_overdue_overlay.index(idx) + 1
                                    ds_disp = index_draws_since_overlay.get(idx, \"\")
                                    badge_html = f'<sup class=\"rank-badge\">{rank_num} ({ds_disp})</sup>'
                                elif idx_style.get(\"bg\") == \"red\":
                                    row_class = \"row-red\"
                                    rank_value = idx_style.get(\"rank\")
                                    badge_html = f'<sup class=\"rank-badge\">{rank_value}</sup>' if rank_value else \"\"
                            def wrap_cell(content):
                                return f\"<div class='{row_class}'>{content}</div>\" if row_class else content
                            index_cell = wrap_cell(f\"{idx}{badge_html}\")
                            singles_cell = wrap_cell(f\"{s_html}{badge_html}\")
                            doubles_cell = wrap_cell(f\"{d_html}{badge_html}\")
                            rows.append({\"Index\": index_cell, \"Singles\": singles_cell, \"Doubles\": doubles_cell})
                        if rows:
                            df_v = pd.DataFrame(rows)
                            st.markdown(df_v.to_html(escape=False, index=False), unsafe_allow_html=True)
                        else:
                            st.caption(\"V-TRAC table unavailable\")

                    def _render_overdue_pairs(payload):
                        data = payload.get(\"data\", {})
                        nonrep = data.get(\"nonrep\") or {}
                        rep = data.get(\"rep\") or {}
                        THR_R_RED = 107
                        THR_R_BLUE = 71
                        THR_NR_RED = 56
                        THR_NR_BLUE = 37
                        THR_PENDING = 25
                        rep_red = \", \".join(sorted(p for p, ds in rep.items() if ds >= THR_R_RED)) or \"None\"
                        rep_blue = \", \".join(sorted(p for p, ds in rep.items() if THR_R_BLUE <= ds < THR_R_RED)) or
        \"None\"
                        rep_purple = \", \".join(sorted(p for p, ds in rep.items() if THR_PENDING <= ds < THR_R_BLUE))
        or \"None\"
                        nr_red = \", \".join(sorted(p for p, ds in nonrep.items() if ds >= THR_NR_RED)) or \"None\"
                        nr_blue = \", \".join(sorted(p for p, ds in nonrep.items() if THR_NR_BLUE <= ds < THR_NR_RED))
        or \"None\"
                        nr_purple = \", \".join(sorted(p for p, ds in nonrep.items() if THR_PENDING <= ds <
        THR_NR_BLUE)) or \"None\"
                        st.markdown(\"**Overdue Pairs**\")
                        st.markdown(f\"- **Repeating** — RED: {rep_red}<br>- BLUE: {rep_blue}<br>- PURPLE:
        {rep_purple}\", unsafe_allow_html=True)
                        st.markdown(f\"- **Non-Repeating** — RED: {nr_red}<br>- BLUE: {nr_blue}<br>- PURPLE:
        {nr_purple}\", unsafe_allow_html=True)

                    def _render_four_panels(payload):
                        data = payload.get(\"data\", {})
                        draws = data.get(\"draws\") or []
                        pair_status = data.get(\"pair_status\") or {}
                        vstat = data.get(\"vstat\") or {}
                        sums_stats = data.get(\"sums_stats\") or {}
                        combos_stats = vstat.get(0, {})
                        singles_ds = combos_stats.get(\"singles_ds\", {}) if isinstance(combos_stats, dict) else {}
                        doubles_ds = combos_stats.get(\"doubles_ds\", {}) if isinstance(combos_stats, dict) else {}
                        st.markdown(\"**Latest Draws**\")
                        if draws:
                            st.dataframe(pd.DataFrame({\"Draw\": draws[:5]}), use_container_width=True, hide_index=True)
                        else:
                            st.caption(\"No draws\")
                        st.markdown(\"**Pairs Analysis Results**\")
                        times_drawn: Dict[str, int] = {}
                        for i, d in enumerate(draws[:150]):
                            if not isinstance(d, str) or len(d) "'!= 3:
                                continue
                            d1, d2, d3 = d[0], d[1], d[2]
                            for raw in (d1 + d2, d2 + d3, d1 + d3):
                                p = "".join(sorted(raw))
                                times_drawn[p] = times_drawn.get(p, 0) + 1
                        all_pairs = sorted(set(list(times_drawn.keys()) + list(pair_status.keys())))
                        if all_pairs:
                            pair_rows = []
                            for p in all_pairs:
                                pair_rows.append({
                                    "Pair": p,
                                    "Times Drawn": times_drawn.get(p, 0),
                                    "Draws Since": pair_status.get(p, {}).get("ds", 0) if isinstance(pair_status.get(p),
        dict) else pair_status.get(p, 0),
                                })
                            st.dataframe(pd.DataFrame(pair_rows).sort_values("Draws Since", ascending=False),
        use_container_width=True, hide_index=True)
                        else:
                            st.caption("No pair data")
                        st.markdown("**Combinations Analysis (Draws Since)**")
                        safe_rows = []
                        for combo_map, combo_type in ((singles_ds, "Single"), (doubles_ds, "Double")):
                            for base, ds in combo_map.items():
                                status = {}
                                if combo_type == "Single":
                                    if ds >= 501:
                                        status[base] = {"shape_red_circle": True}
                                    elif ds >= 334:
                                        status[base] = {"shape_blue_square": True}
                                else:
                                    if ds >= 1000:
                                        status[base] = {"shape_red_circle": True}
                                    elif ds >= 667:
                                        status[base] = {"shape_blue_square": True}
                                html_combo = _format_combo(str(base).zfill(3), status, pair_status)
                                safe_rows.append({"Combo": html_combo, "Type": combo_type, "Draws Since": int(ds)})
                        if safe_rows:
                            dfc_html = pd.DataFrame(sorted(safe_rows, key=lambda x: x["Draws Since"], reverse=True))
                            st.markdown("<div style='"'max-height: 280px; overflow-y: auto;'>\" +
        dfc_html.to_html(escape=False, index=False) + \"</div>\", unsafe_allow_html=True)
                        else:
                            st.caption(\"No combination data\")
                        if callable(_build_sums_df) and isinstance(sums_stats, dict) and sums_stats.get(\"by_sum\"):
                            try:
                                df_sums = _build_sums_df(sums_stats)
                                st.markdown(\"**Sums Tracking**\")
                                st.markdown(\"<div style='max-height: 280px; overflow-y: auto;'>\" +
        df_sums.to_html(escape=False, index=False) + \"</div>\", unsafe_allow_html=True)
                            except Exception:
                                st.caption(\"Sums tracking unavailable\")

                    def _render_vtrac_index_hits(payload):
                        data = payload.get(\"data\", {})
                        vstat = data.get(\"vstat\") or {}
                        draws = data.get(\"draws\") or []

        else:
            temp_payloads: List[dict] = []

            for option_label, option_key in variant_options:
                cached_variant = cached_aux_analysis(state, option_key)
                variant_cache[option_key] = cached_variant
                if not cached_variant:

                if not variant_draws:

                cached_variant["sums_stats"] = sums_stats_variant
                draws_by_variant[option_key] = variant_draws
                temp_payloads.append({"label": option_label, "key": option_key, "data": cached_variant})
            if not temp_payloads:

            else:
                try:

                        draws_by_variant,

                    )
                except Exception as _pos_err:
                    st.warning(f"Positional analysis unavailable: {_pos_err}")
                else:
                    variant_payloads[:] = temp_payloads
                    tracker_css = "<style>.pos-tracker{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;margin-bottom:8px;}" '"\\
                        + \".pos-tracker th,.pos-tracker td{border:1px solid
        #ddd;padding:4px;text-align:center;font-size:0.95em;}\" \\
                        + \".pos-tracker th{background-color:#f5f5f5;}\" + \".row-green{background-color:#e9f7ef;}\" \\
                        + \".row-red{background-color:#fdecea;}\" + \".rank-badge{font-size:0.75em;margin-left:4px;}\" +
        \"</style>\"
                    st.markdown(tracker_css, unsafe_allow_html=True)
                    order = [(\"Combined\", \"combined\"), (\"Midday\", \"midday\"), (\"Evening\", \"evening\")]
                    st.subheader(\"Positional Tracker\")
                    for payload in variant_payloads:
                        payload[\"pos_result\"] = report.variant_results.get(payload[\"key\"])
                    for display_label, variant_key in order:
                        payload = next((p for p in variant_payloads if p[\"key\"] == variant_key), None)
                        if not payload:

                        if not pos_result or not getattr(pos_result, \"tracker_grid\", None):
                            continue
                        _render_tracker_table(f\"{state}_{display_label}\", pos_result.tracker_grid,
        pos_result.draws_used, payload[\"data\"].get(\"source\"))
                    if report.consensus_notes:
                        st.markdown(\"**Cross-variant pressure summary**\")
                        for note in report.consensus_notes:
                            st.markdown(f\"- {note}\")
                    aggregated = report.aggregated_digits or {}
                    if aggregated:
                        summary_cols = st.columns(3)
                        for pos_idx, column in enumerate(summary_cols):
                            with column:
                                st.markdown(f\"P{pos_idx + 1}\")
                                agg_items = aggregated.get(pos_idx, [])
                                if not agg_items:
                                    st.caption(\"No data\")
                                    continue

                                for agg in agg_items[:5]:
                                    rows.append(

    from textwrap import dedent

    new_block = dedent(\"\"\"
    variant_cache: Dict[str, Optional[dict]] = {}
    variant_payloads: List[dict] = []

    def _render_tracker_table(title, tracker_grid, draws_used, source_path):
        tracker_header = \"<thead><tr><th>Rank</th><th>P1 DS</th><th>P1 DIG</th><th>P2 DS</th><th>P2 DIG</th><th>P3 DS</
        th><th>P3 DIG</th></tr></thead>\"
        body_rows = []
        for rank in range(3):
            row_cells = [f\"<td>{rank + 1}</td>\"]
            for pos_idx in range(3):
                cells = tracker_grid.get(pos_idx, [])
                if rank < len(cells):
                    cell = cells[rank]
                    ds_html = cell.draws_since
                    digit_style = \"background-color:#ffea70;padding:0 6px;\"
                    if cell.hard_due:
                        digit_style += \"color:#d60000;font-weight:600;\"
                    digit_html = f\"<span style='{digit_style}'>{cell.digit}</span>\"
                else:
                    ds_html = \"&mdash;\"
                    digit_html = \"<span style='background-color:#ffea70;padding:0 6px;'>&mdash;</span>\"
                row_cells.append(f\"<td>{ds_html}</td>\")
                row_cells.append(f\"<td>{digit_html}</td>\")
            body_rows.append(\"<tr>\" + \"\".join(row_cells) + \"</tr>\")
        table_html = (
            \"<table class='pos-tracker'>\"
            + tracker_header
            + \"<tbody>\"
            + \"\".join(body_rows)
            + \"</tbody></table>\"
        )
        st.markdown(table_html, unsafe_allow_html=True)
        meta_bits: List[str] = []
        if source_path:
            try:
                meta_bits.append(f\"Draw source: {_Path(source_path).name}\")
            except Exception:
                meta_bits.append(f\"Draw source: {source_path}\")
        if draws_used:
            meta_bits.append(f\"Draws used: {draws_used}\")
        if meta_bits:
            st.caption(\" | \".join(meta_bits))


    def _render_vtrac_box(payload):
        data = payload.get(\"data\", {})
        vstat = data.get(\"vstat\") or {}
        if not vstat:
            st.caption(\"No V-TRAC data\")
            return
        pair_status = data.get(\"pair_status\") or {}
        draws = data.get(\"draws\") or []
        draws_1000 = data.get(\"draws_1000\") or draws
        sums_stats = data.get(\"sums_stats\") or {}
        index_first_seen: Dict[int, int] = {}
        total_len = len(draws_1000)
        try:
            for idx, draw_value in enumerate(draws_1000):
                if not isinstance(draw_value, str) or len(draw_value) "'!= 3 or len(set(draw_value)) == 1:
                    continue
                idx_tmp = get_vtrac_index(draw_value)
                if idx_tmp and idx_tmp not in index_first_seen:
                    index_first_seen[idx_tmp] = idx
        except Exception:
            index_first_seen = {}
        index_draws_since_overlay = {i: index_first_seen.get(i, total_len) for i in range(1, 36)}
        sorted_overlay = sorted(index_draws_since_overlay.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1], reverse=True)
        top10_overlay = [i for i, _ds in sorted_overlay[:10]]
        rows = []
        for entry in VTRAC_DISPLAY:
            idx = entry["Index"]
            singles = entry.get("Singles", "").split()
            doubles = entry.get("Doubles", "").split()
            sdict = vstat.get(idx, {}).get("singles_status", {})
            ddict = vstat.get(idx, {}).get("doubles_status", {})
            s_html = " ".join([
                (_format_combo(combo, sdict, pair_status) + _sums_badge_for(combo, sums_stats))
                for combo in singles
            ]) if singles else "&nbsp;"
            d_html = " ".join([
                (_format_combo(combo, ddict, pair_status) + _sums_badge_for(combo, sums_stats))
                for combo in doubles
            ]) if doubles else "&nbsp;"
            idx_style = vstat.get(idx, {}).get("index_style", {})
            row_class = ""
            badge_html = ""
            if idx_style.get("bg") == "green":
                row_class = "row-green"
                rank_value = idx_style.get("rank")
                badge_html = f"<sup class='"'rank-badge'>{rank_value}</sup>\" if rank_value else \"\"
            else:
                if idx in top10_overlay:
                    row_class = \"row-red\"
                    rank_num = top10_overlay.index(idx) + 1
                    ds_disp = index_draws_since_overlay.get(idx, \"\")
                    badge_html = f\"<sup class='rank-badge'>{rank_num} ({ds_disp})</sup>\"
                elif idx_style.get(\"bg\") == \"red\":
                    row_class = \"row-red\"
                    rank_value = idx_style.get(\"rank\")
                    badge_html = f\"<sup class='rank-badge'>{rank_value}</sup>\" if rank_value else \"\"
            def wrap_cell(content: str) -> str:
                return f\"<div class='{row_class}'>{content}</div>\" if row_class else content
            index_cell = wrap_cell(f\"{idx}{badge_html}\")
            singles_cell = wrap_cell(f\"{s_html}{badge_html}\")
            doubles_cell = wrap_cell(f\"{d_html}{badge_html}\")
            rows.append({\"Index\": index_cell, \"Singles\": singles_cell, \"Doubles\": doubles_cell})
        if rows:
            df_v = pd.DataFrame(rows)
            st.markdown(df_v.to_html(escape=False, index=False), unsafe_allow_html=True)
        else:
            st.caption(\"V-TRAC table unavailable\")


    def _render_overdue_pairs(payload):
        data = payload.get(\"data\", {})
        nonrep = data.get(\"nonrep\") or {}
        rep = data.get(\"rep\") or {}
        thr_r_red = 107
        thr_r_blue = 71
        thr_nr_red = 56
        thr_nr_blue = 37
        thr_pending = 25
        rep_red = \", \".join(sorted(p for p, ds in rep.items() if ds >= thr_r_red)) or \"None\"
        rep_blue = \", \".join(sorted(p for p, ds in rep.items() if thr_r_blue <= ds < thr_r_red)) or \"None\"
        rep_purple = \", \".join(sorted(p for p, ds in rep.items() if thr_pending <= ds < thr_r_blue)) or \"None\"
        nr_red = \", \".join(sorted(p for p, ds in nonrep.items() if ds >= thr_nr_red)) or \"None\"
        nr_blue = \", \".join(sorted(p for p, ds in nonrep.items() if thr_nr_blue <= ds < thr_nr_red)) or \"None\"
        nr_purple = \", \".join(sorted(p for p, ds in nonrep.items() if thr_pending <= ds < thr_nr_blue)) or \"None\"
        st.markdown(\"**Overdue Pairs**\")
        st.markdown(f\"- **Repeating** — RED: {rep_red}<br>- BLUE: {rep_blue}<br>- PURPLE: {rep_purple}\",
        unsafe_allow_html=True)
        st.markdown(f\"- **Non-Repeating** — RED: {nr_red}<br>- BLUE: {nr_blue}<br>- PURPLE: {nr_purple}\",
        unsafe_allow_html=True)


    def _render_four_panels(payload):
        data = payload.get(\"data\", {})
        draws = data.get(\"draws\") or []
        pair_status = data.get(\"pair_status\") or {}
        vstat = data.get(\"vstat\") or {}
        sums_stats = data.get(\"sums_stats\") or {}
        combos_stats = vstat.get(0, {}) if isinstance(vstat, dict) else {}
        singles_ds = combos_stats.get(\"singles_ds\", {}) if isinstance(combos_stats, dict) else {}
        doubles_ds = combos_stats.get(\"doubles_ds\", {}) if isinstance(combos_stats, dict) else {}
        st.markdown(\"**Latest Draws**\")
        if draws:
            st.dataframe(pd.DataFrame({\"Draw\": draws[:5]}), use_container_width=True, hide_index=True)
        else:
            st.caption(\"No draws\")
        st.markdown(\"**Pairs Analysis Results**\")
        times_drawn: Dict[str, int] = {}
        for d in draws[:150]:
            if not isinstance(d, str) or len(d) "'!= 3:
                continue
            d1, d2, d3 = d[0], d[1], d[2]
            for raw in (d1 + d2, d2 + d3, d1 + d3):
                p = "".join(sorted(raw))
                times_drawn[p] = times_drawn.get(p, 0) + 1
        all_pairs = sorted(set(times_drawn.keys()) | set(pair_status.keys()))
        if all_pairs:
            pair_rows = []
            for p in all_pairs:
                overdue_val = pair_status.get(p)
                if isinstance(overdue_val, dict):
                    overdue = overdue_val.get("ds", 0)
                else:
                    overdue = overdue_val or 0
                pair_rows.append({"Pair": p, "Times Drawn": times_drawn.get(p, 0), "Draws Since": overdue})
            st.dataframe(pd.DataFrame(pair_rows).sort_values("Draws Since", ascending=False), use_container_width=True,
        hide_index=True)
        else:
            st.caption("No pair data")
        st.markdown("**Combinations Analysis (Draws Since)**")
        safe_rows = []
        for combo_map, combo_type in ((singles_ds, "Single"), (doubles_ds, "Double")):
            for base, ds in combo_map.items():
                status = {}
                if combo_type == "Single":
                    if ds >= 501:
                        status[base] = {"shape_red_circle": True}
                    elif ds >= 334:
                        status[base] = {"shape_blue_square": True}
                else:
                    if ds >= 1000:
                        status[base] = {"shape_red_circle": True}
                    elif ds >= 667:
                        status[base] = {"shape_blue_square": True}
                html_combo = _format_combo(str(base).zfill(3), status, pair_status)
                safe_rows.append({"Combo": html_combo, "Type": combo_type, "Draws Since": int(ds)})
        if safe_rows:
            dfc_html = pd.DataFrame(sorted(safe_rows, key=lambda x: x["Draws Since"], reverse=True))
            st.markdown("<div style='"'max-height: 280px; overflow-y: auto;'>\" + dfc_html.to_html(escape=False,
        index=False) + \"</div>\", unsafe_allow_html=True)
        else:
            st.caption(\"No combination data\")
        if callable(_build_sums_df) and isinstance(sums_stats, dict) and sums_stats.get(\"by_sum\"):
            try:
                df_sums = _build_sums_df(sums_stats)
                st.markdown(\"**Sums Tracking**\")
                st.markdown(\"<div style='max-height: 280px; overflow-y: auto;'>\" + df_sums.to_html(escape=False,
        index=False) + \"</div>\", unsafe_allow_html=True)
            except Exception:
                st.caption(\"Sums tracking unavailable\")


    def _render_vtrac_index_hits(payload):
        data = payload.get(\"data\", {})
        vstat = data.get(\"vstat\") or {}
        draws = data.get(\"draws\") or []
        draws_1000 = data.get(\"draws_1000\") or draws
        index_first_seen: Dict[int, int] = {}
        total_len = len(draws_1000)
        for i, d in enumerate(draws_1000):
            if not isinstance(d, str) or len(d) "'!= 3 or len(set(d)) == 1:
                continue
            idx = get_vtrac_index(d)
            if idx and idx not in index_first_seen:
                index_first_seen[idx] = i
        index_draws_since = {i: index_first_seen.get(i, total_len) for i in range(1, 36)}
        sorted_ds = sorted(index_draws_since.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1], reverse=True)
        top10_overdue = [i for i, _ in sorted_ds[:10]]
        rows = []
        for idx, ds in index_draws_since.items():
            style = vstat.get(idx, {}).get("index_style", {}) if isinstance(vstat, dict) else {}
            status_label = "Tracking"
            if idx in top10_overdue:
                status_label = "Overdue"
            elif style.get("bg") == "green":
                status_label = "Recent"
            rows.append({"Index": idx, "Draws Since": ds, "Status": status_label})
        if rows:
            df_idx = pd.DataFrame(rows)
            st.markdown("**V-TRAC Index Hits**")
            st.dataframe(df_idx.sort_values(["Status", "Draws Since"], ascending=[True, False]),
        use_container_width=True, hide_index=True)
        else:
            st.caption("No V-TRAC index data")


    def _render_variant_column(payload):
        label = payload.get("label", "Variant")
        st.markdown(f"#### {label}")
        data = payload.get("data", {})
        source_path = data.get("source")
        if source_path:
            try:
                st.caption(f"Draw source: {_Path(source_path).name}")
            except Exception:
                st.caption(f"Draw source: {source_path}")
        _render_vtrac_box(payload)
        _render_overdue_pairs(payload)
        _render_four_panels(payload)
        _render_vtrac_index_hits(payload)

    with st.expander("Positional Tracker & Variant Stack", expanded=True):
        pos_window = st.slider(
            "Window (draws scanned)",
            min_value=60,
            max_value=360,
            value=150,
            step=10,
            key=f"aux_pos_window_{state_key}_{selected_variant_key}",
        )
        pos_topk = st.radio(
            "Top-K per position",
            options=[3, 4, 5],
            index=0,
            horizontal=True,
            key=f"aux_pos_topk_{state_key}_{selected_variant_key}",
        )
        try:
            with _project_modules_first():
                positional_tool = _load_positional_tool_real()
        except Exception as _pos_load_err:
            positional_tool = None
            st.caption(f"Positional module unavailable: {_pos_load_err}")
        else:
            temp_payloads: List[dict] = []
            draws_by_variant: Dict[str, List[str]] = {}
            for option_label, option_key in variant_options:
                cached_variant = cached_aux_analysis(state, option_key)
                variant_cache[option_key] = cached_variant
                if not cached_variant:
                    continue
                variant_draws = cached_variant.get("draws") or []
                if not variant_draws:
                    continue
                if callable(_calc_sums):
                    if option_key == selected_variant_key:
                        sums_stats_variant = sums_stats
                    else:
                        try:
                            sums_stats_variant = _calc_sums(variant_draws, window=analysis_draws)
                        except Exception:
                            sums_stats_variant = {"window": 0, "by_sum": {}, "by_root_sum": {}}
                else:
                    sums_stats_variant = {"window": 0, "by_sum": {}, "by_root_sum": {}}
                cached_variant["sums_stats"] = sums_stats_variant
                draws_by_variant[option_key] = variant_draws
                temp_payloads.append({"label": option_label, "key": option_key, "data": cached_variant})
            if not temp_payloads:
                st.caption("No positional draws available across variants.")
            else:
                try:
                    report = positional_tool.analyze_state_variants(
                        draws_by_variant,
                        window=int(pos_window),
                        topk=int(pos_topk),
                        due_doubles_active=due_doubles_flag,
                    )
                except Exception as _pos_err:
                    st.warning(f"Positional analysis unavailable: {_pos_err}")
                else:
                    variant_payloads[:] = temp_payloads
                    tracker_css = (
                        "<style>.pos-tracker{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;margin-bottom:8px;}"
                        ".pos-tracker th,.pos-tracker td{border:1px solid
        #ddd;padding:4px;text-align:center;font-size:0.95em;}"
                        ".pos-tracker th{background-color:#f5f5f5;}"
                        ".row-green{background-color:#e9f7ef;}"
                        ".row-red{background-color:#fdecea;}"
                        ".rank-badge{font-size:0.75em;margin-left:4px;}</style>"
                    )
                    st.markdown(tracker_css, unsafe_allow_html=True)
                    order = [("Combined", "combined"), ("Midday", "midday"), ("Evening", "evening")]
                    st.subheader("Positional Tracker")
                    for payload in variant_payloads:
                        payload["pos_result"] = report.variant_results.get(payload["key"])
                    for display_label, variant_key in order:
                        payload = next((p for p in variant_payloads if p["key"] == variant_key), None)
                        if not payload:
                            continue
                        pos_result = payload.get("pos_result")
                        if not pos_result or not getattr(pos_result, "tracker_grid", None):
                            continue
                        _render_tracker_table(
                            f"{state}_{display_label}",
                            pos_result.tracker_grid,
                            pos_result.draws_used,
                            payload["data"].get("source"),
                        )
                    if report.consensus_notes:
                        st.markdown("**Cross-variant pressure summary**")
                        for note in report.consensus_notes:
                            st.markdown(f"- {note}")
                    aggregated = report.aggregated_digits or {}
                    if aggregated:

                        for pos_idx, column in enumerate(summary_cols):
                            with column:
                                st.markdown(f"P{pos_idx + 1}")

        agg.occurrences)

                                            "Tags": " ".join(sorted(agg.tags)),

                                _render_variant_column(payload)

            else:
                if idx in top10_overlay:
                    row_class = \"row-red\"
                    rank_num = top10_overlay.index(idx) + 1
                    ds_disp = index_draws_since_overlay.get(idx, \"\")
                    badge_html = f\"<sup class='rank-badge'>{rank_num} ({ds_disp})</sup>\"
                elif idx_style.get(\"bg\") == \"red\":
                    row_class = \"row-red\"
                    rank_value = idx_style.get(\"rank\")
                    badge_html = f\"<sup class='rank-badge'>{rank_value}</sup>\" if rank_value else \"\"
            def wrap_cell(content):
                return f\"<div class='{row_class}'>{content}</div>\" if row_class else content
            index_cell = wrap_cell(f\"{idx}{badge_html}\")
            singles_cell = wrap_cell(f\"{s_html}{badge_html}\")
            doubles_cell = wrap_cell(f\"{d_html}{badge_html}\")
            rows.append({\"Index\": index_cell, \"Singles\": singles_cell, \"Doubles\": doubles_cell})
        if rows:
            df_v = pd.DataFrame(rows)
            st.markdown(df_v.to_html(escape=False, index=False), unsafe_allow_html=True)
        else:
            st.caption(\"V-TRAC table unavailable\")


    def _render_overdue_pairs(payload):
        data = payload.get(\"data\", {})
        nonrep = data.get(\"nonrep\") or {}
        rep = data.get(\"rep\") or {}
        thr_r_red = 107
        thr_r_blue = 71
        thr_nr_red = 56
        thr_nr_blue = 37
        thr_pending = 25
        rep_red = \", \".join(sorted(p for p, ds in rep.items() if ds >= thr_r_red)) or \"None\"
        rep_blue = \", \".join(sorted(p for p, ds in rep.items() if thr_r_blue <= ds < thr_r_red)) or \"None\"
        rep_purple = \", \".join(sorted(p for p, ds in rep.items() if thr_pending <= ds < thr_r_blue)) or \"None\"
        nr_red = \", \".join(sorted(p for p, ds in nonrep.items() if ds >= thr_nr_red)) or \"None\"
        nr_blue = \", \".join(sorted(p for p, ds in nonrep.items() if thr_nr_blue <= ds < thr_nr_red)) or \"None\"
        nr_purple = \", \".join(sorted(p for p, ds in nonrep.items() if thr_pending <= ds < thr_nr_blue)) or \"None\"
        st.markdown(\"**Overdue Pairs**\")
        st.markdown(f\"- **Repeating** — RED: {rep_red}<br>- BLUE: {rep_blue}<br>- PURPLE: {rep_purple}\",
        unsafe_allow_html=True)
        st.markdown(f\"- **Non-Repeating** — RED: {nr_red}<br>- BLUE: {nr_blue}<br>- PURPLE: {nr_purple}\",
        unsafe_allow_html=True)


    def _render_four_panels(payload):
        data = payload.get(\"data\", {})
        draws = data.get(\"draws\") or []
        pair_status = data.get(\"pair_status\") or {}
        vstat = data.get(\"vstat\") or {}
        sums_stats = data.get(\"sums_stats\") or {}
        combos_stats = vstat.get(0, {}) if isinstance(vstat, dict) else {}
        singles_ds = combos_stats.get(\"singles_ds\", {}) if isinstance(combos_stats, dict) else {}
        doubles_ds = combos_stats.get(\"doubles_ds\", {}) if isinstance(combos_stats, dict) else {}
        st.markdown(\"**Latest Draws**\")
        if draws:
            st.dataframe(pd.DataFrame({\"Draw\": draws[:5]}), use_container_width=True, hide_index=True)
        else:
            st.caption(\"No draws\")
        st.markdown(\"**Pairs Analysis Results**\")
        times_drawn = {}
        for d in draws[:150]:
            if not isinstance(d, str) or len(d) "'!= 3:
                continue
            d1, d2, d3 = d[0], d[1], d[2]
            for raw in (d1 + d2, d2 + d3, d1 + d3):
                p = "".join(sorted(raw))
                times_drawn[p] = times_drawn.get(p, 0) + 1
        all_pairs = sorted(set(times_drawn.keys()) | set(pair_status.keys()))
        if all_pairs:
            pair_rows = []
            for p in all_pairs:
                overdue_val = pair_status.get(p)
                if isinstance(overdue_val, dict):
                    overdue = overdue_val.get("ds", 0)
                else:
                    overdue = overdue_val or 0
                pair_rows.append({"Pair": p, "Times Drawn": times_drawn.get(p, 0), "Draws Since": overdue})
            st.dataframe(pd.DataFrame(pair_rows).sort_values("Draws Since", ascending=False), use_container_width=True,
        hide_index=True)
        else:
            st.caption("No pair data")
        st.markdown("**Combinations Analysis (Draws Since)**")
        safe_rows = []
        for combo_map, combo_type in ((singles_ds, "Single"), (doubles_ds, "Double")):
            for base, ds in combo_map.items():
                status = {}
                if combo_type == "Single":
                    if ds >= 501:
                        status[base] = {"shape_red_circle": True}
                    elif ds >= 334:
                        status[base] = {"shape_blue_square": True}
                else:
                    if ds >= 1000:
                        status[base] = {"shape_red_circle": True}
                    elif ds >= 667:
                        status[base] = {"shape_blue_square": True}
                html_combo = _format_combo(str(base).zfill(3), status, pair_status)
                safe_rows.append({"Combo": html_combo, "Type": combo_type, "Draws Since": int(ds)})
        if safe_rows:
            dfc_html = pd.DataFrame(sorted(safe_rows, key=lambda x: x["Draws Since"], reverse=True))
            st.markdown("<div style='"'max-height: 280px; overflow-y: auto;'>\" + dfc_html.to_html(escape=False,
        index=False) + \"</div>\", unsafe_allow_html=True)
        else:
            st.caption(\"No combination data\")
        if callable(_build_sums_df) and isinstance(sums_stats, dict) and sums_stats.get(\"by_sum\"):
            try:
                df_sums = _build_sums_df(sums_stats)
                st.markdown(\"**Sums Tracking**\")
                st.markdown(\"<div style='max-height: 280px; overflow-y: auto;'>\" + df_sums.to_html(escape=False,
        index=False) + \"</div>\", unsafe_allow_html=True)
            except Exception:
                st.caption(\"Sums tracking unavailable\")


    def _render_vtrac_index_hits(payload):
        data = payload.get(\"data\", {})
        vstat = data.get(\"vstat\") or {}
        draws = data.get(\"draws\") or []
        draws_1000 = data.get(\"draws_1000\") or draws
        index_first_seen = {}
        total_len = len(draws_1000)
        for i, d in enumerate(draws_1000):
            if not isinstance(d, str) or len(d) "'!= 3 or len(set(d)) == 1:
                continue
            idx = get_vtrac_index(d)
            if idx and idx not in index_first_seen:
                index_first_seen[idx] = i
        index_draws_since = {i: index_first_seen.get(i, total_len) for i in range(1, 36)}
        sorted_ds = sorted(index_draws_since.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1], reverse=True)
        top10_overdue = [i for i, _ in sorted_ds[:10]]
        rows = []
        for idx, ds in index_draws_since.items():
            style = vstat.get(idx, {}).get("index_style", {}) if isinstance(vstat, dict) else {}
            status_label = "Tracking"
            if idx in top10_overdue:
                status_label = "Overdue"
            elif style.get("bg") == "green":
                status_label = "Recent"
            rows.append({"Index": idx, "Draws Since": ds, "Status": status_label})
        if rows:
            df_idx = pd.DataFrame(rows)
            st.markdown("**V-TRAC Index Hits**")
            st.dataframe(df_idx.sort_values(["Status", "Draws Since"], ascending=[True, False]),
        use_container_width=True, hide_index=True)
        else:
            st.caption("No V-TRAC index data")

        source_path = data.get("source")

            try:
                st.caption(f"Draw source: {_Path(source_path).name}")
            except Exception:

    def _render_tracker_table(title, tracker_grid, draws_used, source_path):
        tracker_header = \"<thead><tr><th>Rank</th><th>P1 DS</th><th>P1 DIG</th><th>P2 DS</th><th>P2 DIG</th><th>P3 DS</
        th><th>P3 DIG</th></tr></thead>\"
        body_rows = []
        for rank in range(3):
            row_cells = [f\"<td>{rank + 1}</td>\"]
            for pos_idx in range(3):
                cells = tracker_grid.get(pos_idx, [])
                if rank < len(cells):
                    cell = cells[rank]
                    ds_html = cell.draws_since
                    digit_style = \"background-color:#ffea70;padding:0 6px;\"
                    if cell.hard_due:
                        digit_style += \"color:#d60000;font-weight:600;\"
                    digit_html = f\"<span style='{digit_style}'>{cell.digit}</span>\"
                else:
                    ds_html = \"&mdash;\"
                    digit_html = \"<span style='background-color:#ffea70;padding:0 6px;'>&mdash;</span>\"
                row_cells.append(f\"<td>{ds_html}</td>\")
                row_cells.append(f\"<td>{digit_html}</td>\")
            body_rows.append(\"<tr>\" + \"\".join(row_cells) + \"</tr>\")
        table_html = (
            \"<table class='pos-tracker'>\"
            + tracker_header
            + \"<tbody>\"
            + \"\".join(body_rows)
            + \"</tbody></table>\"
        )
        st.markdown(table_html, unsafe_allow_html=True)
        meta_bits = []
        if source_path:
            try:
                meta_bits.append(f\"Draw source: {_Path(source_path).name}\")
            except Exception:
                meta_bits.append(f\"Draw source: {source_path}\")
        if draws_used:
            meta_bits.append(f\"Draws used: {draws_used}\")
        if meta_bits:
            st.caption(\" | \".join(meta_bits))


    def _render_vtrac_box(payload):
        data = payload.get(\"data\", {})
        vstat = data.get(\"vstat\") or {}
        if not vstat:
            st.caption(\"No V-TRAC data\")
            return
        pair_status = data.get(\"pair_status\") or {}
        draws = data.get(\"draws\") or []
        draws_1000 = data.get(\"draws_1000\") or draws
        sums_stats = data.get(\"sums_stats\") or {}
        index_first_seen = {}
        total_len = len(draws_1000)
        try:
            for idx, draw_value in enumerate(draws_1000):
                if not isinstance(draw_value, str) or len(draw_value) "'!= 3 or len(set(draw_value)) == 1:
                    continue
                idx_tmp = get_vtrac_index(draw_value)
                if idx_tmp and idx_tmp not in index_first_seen:
                    index_first_seen[idx_tmp] = idx
        except Exception:
            index_first_seen = {}
        index_draws_since_overlay = {i: index_first_seen.get(i, total_len) for i in range(1, 36)}
        sorted_overlay = sorted(index_draws_since_overlay.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1], reverse=True)
        top10_overlay = [i for i, _ds in sorted_overlay[:10]]
        rows = []
        for entry in VTRAC_DISPLAY:
            idx = entry["Index"]
            singles = entry.get("Singles", "").split()
            doubles = entry.get("Doubles", "").split()
            sdict = vstat.get(idx, {}).get("singles_status", {})
            ddict = vstat.get(idx, {}).get("doubles_status", {})
            s_html = " ".join([
                (_format_combo(combo, sdict, pair_status) + _sums_badge_for(combo, sums_stats))
                for combo in singles
            ]) if singles else "&nbsp;"
            d_html = " ".join([
                (_format_combo(combo, ddict, pair_status) + _sums_badge_for(combo, sums_stats))
                for combo in doubles
            ]) if doubles else "&nbsp;"
            idx_style = vstat.get(idx, {}).get("index_style", {})
            row_class = ""
            badge_html = ""
            if idx_style.get("bg") == "green":
                row_class = "row-green"
                rank_value = idx_style.get("rank")
                badge_html = f"<sup class='"'rank-badge'>{rank_value}</sup>\" if rank_value else \"\"
            else:
                if idx in top10_overlay:
                    row_class = \"row-red\"
                    rank_num = top10_overlay.index(idx) + 1
                    ds_disp = index_draws_since_overlay.get(idx, \"\")
                    badge_html = f\"<sup class='rank-badge'>{rank_num} ({ds_disp})</sup>\"
                elif idx_style.get(\"bg\") == \"red\":
                    row_class = \"row-red\"
                    rank_value = idx_style.get(\"rank\")
                    badge_html = f\"<sup class='rank-badge'>{rank_value}</sup>\" if rank_value else \"\"
            def wrap_cell(content):
                return f\"<div class='{row_class}'>{content}</div>\" if row_class else content
            index_cell = wrap_cell(f\"{idx}{badge_html}\")
            singles_cell = wrap_cell(f\"{s_html}{badge_html}\")
            doubles_cell = wrap_cell(f\"{d_html}{badge_html}\")
            rows.append({\"Index\": index_cell, \"Singles\": singles_cell, \"Doubles\": doubles_cell})
        if rows:
            df_v = pd.DataFrame(rows)
            st.markdown(df_v.to_html(escape=False, index=False), unsafe_allow_html=True)
        else:
            st.caption(\"V-TRAC table unavailable\")

        pos_window = st.slider(

            options=[3, 4, 5],
            index=0,
            horizontal=True,
            key=f\"aux_pos_topk_{state_key}_{selected_variant_key}\",
        )
        try:
            with _project_modules_first():
                positional_tool = _load_positional_tool_real()
        except Exception as _pos_load_err:
            positional_tool = None
            st.caption(f\"Positional module unavailable: {_pos_load_err}\")
        else:
            temp_payloads: List[dict] = []
            draws_by_variant: Dict[str, List[str]] = {}
            for option_label, option_key in variant_options:
                cached_variant = cached_aux_analysis(state, option_key)
                variant_cache[option_key] = cached_variant
                if not cached_variant:
                    continue
                variant_draws = cached_variant.get(\"draws\") or []
                if not variant_draws:
                    continue
                if callable(_calc_sums):
                    if option_key == selected_variant_key:
                        sums_stats_variant = sums_stats
                    else:
                        try:
                            sums_stats_variant = _calc_sums(variant_draws, window=analysis_draws)
                        except Exception:
                            sums_stats_variant = {\"window\": 0, \"by_sum\": {}, \"by_root_sum\": {}}
                else:
                    sums_stats_variant = {\"window\": 0, \"by_sum\": {}, \"by_root_sum\": {}}
                cached_variant[\"sums_stats\"] = sums_stats_variant
                draws_by_variant[option_key] = variant_draws
                temp_payloads.append({\"label\": option_label, \"key\": option_key, \"data\": cached_variant})
            if not temp_payloads:
                st.caption(\"No positional draws available across variants.\")
            else:
                try:
                    report = positional_tool.analyze_state_variants(
                        draws_by_variant,
                        window=int(pos_window),
                        topk=int(pos_topk),
                        due_doubles_active=due_doubles_flag,
                    )
                except Exception as _pos_err:
                    st.warning(f\"Positional analysis unavailable: {_pos_err}\")
                else:
                    variant_payloads[:] = temp_payloads
                    tracker_css = (
                        \"<style>.pos-tracker{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;margin-bottom:8px;}\"
                        \".pos-tracker th,.pos-tracker td{border:1px solid
        #ddd;padding:4px;text-align:center;font-size:0.95em;}\"
                        \".pos-tracker th{background-color:#f5f5f5;}\"
                        \".row-green{background-color:#e9f7ef;}\"
                        \".row-red{background-color:#fdecea;}\"
                        \".rank-badge{font-size:0.75em;margin-left:4px;}</style>\"
                    )
                    st.markdown(tracker_css, unsafe_allow_html=True)
                    order = [(\"Combined\", \"combined\"), (\"Midday\", \"midday\"), (\"Evening\", \"evening\")]
                    st.subheader(\"Positional Tracker\")
                    for payload in variant_payloads:
                        payload[\"pos_result\"] = report.variant_results.get(payload[\"key\"])
                    for display_label, variant_key in order:
                        payload = next((p for p in variant_payloads if p[\"key\"] == variant_key), None)
                        if not payload:
                            continue
                        pos_result = payload.get(\"pos_result\")
                        if not pos_result or not getattr(pos_result, \"tracker_grid\", None):
                            continue
                        _render_tracker_table(
                            f\"{state}_{display_label}\",
                            pos_result.tracker_grid,
                            pos_result.draws_used,
                            payload[\"data\"].get(\"source\"),
                        )
                    if report.consensus_notes:
                        st.markdown(\"**Cross-variant pressure summary**\")
                        for note in report.consensus_notes:
                            st.markdown(f\"- {note}\")
                    aggregated = report.aggregated_digits or {}
                    if aggregated:
                        summary_cols = st.columns(3)
                        for pos_idx, column in enumerate(summary_cols):
                            with column:
                                st.markdown(f\"P{pos_idx + 1}\")
                                agg_items = aggregated.get(pos_idx, [])
                                if not agg_items:
                                    st.caption(\"No data\")
                                    continue
                                rows = []
                                for agg in agg_items[:5]:
                                    hits = \", \".join(f\"{variant[:1].upper()}#{rank}\" for variant, rank in
        agg.occurrences)
                                    rows.append(
                                        {
                                            \"Digit\": agg.digit,
                                            \"Score\": round(agg.score, 2),
                                            \"Hits\": hits,
                                            \"Tags\": \" \".join(sorted(agg.tags)),
                                        }
                                    )
                                st.dataframe(pd.DataFrame(rows), use_container_width=True, hide_index=True)
                    if report.candidates:
                        st.markdown(\"**Positional shortlist**\")
                        candidate_rows = []
                        for cand in report.candidates:
                            candidate_rows.append(
                                {
                                    \"Combo\": cand.combo,
                                    \"Score\": round(cand.score, 2),
                                    \"Ranks\": \"-\".join(str(r) for r in cand.ranks if r),
                                    \"Root\": cand.digital_root,
                                    \"VTRAC\": \"\" if cand.vtrac_index is None else cand.vtrac_index,
                                    \"Tags\": \" \".join(sorted(cand.tags)),
                                }
                            )
                        st.dataframe(pd.DataFrame(candidate_rows), use_container_width=True, hide_index=True)

    if variant_payloads:
        st.subheader(\"Auxiliary Feature Panels\")
        variant_cols = st.columns(len(variant_payloads))
    '

    def _render_tracker_table(title, tracker_grid, draws_used, source_path):
        tracker_header = \"<thead><tr><th>Rank</th><th>P1 DS</th><th>P1 DIG</th><th>P2 DS</th><th>P2 DIG</th><th>P3 DS</
        th><th>P3 DIG</th></tr></thead>\"
        body_rows = []
        for rank in range(3):
            row_cells = [f\"<td>{rank + 1}</td>\"]
            for pos_idx in range(3):
                cells = tracker_grid.get(pos_idx, [])
                if rank < len(cells):
                    cell = cells[rank]
                    ds_html = cell.draws_since
                    digit_style = \"background-color:#ffea70;padding:0 6px;\"
                    if cell.hard_due:
                        digit_style += \"color:#d60000;font-weight:600;\"
                    digit_html = f\"<span style='{digit_style}'>{cell.digit}</span>\"
                else:
                    ds_html = \"&mdash;\"
                    digit_html = \"<span style='background-color:#ffea70;padding:0 6px;'>&mdash;</span>\"
                row_cells.append(f\"<td>{ds_html}</td>\")
                row_cells.append(f\"<td>{digit_html}</td>\")
            body_rows.append(\"<tr>\" + \"\".join(row_cells) + \"</tr>\")
        table_html = (
            \"<table class='pos-tracker'>\"
            + tracker_header
            + \"<tbody>\"
            + \"\".join(body_rows)
            + \"</tbody></table>\"
        )
        st.markdown(table_html, unsafe_allow_html=True)
        meta_bits = []
        if source_path:
            try:
                meta_bits.append(f\"Draw source: {_Path(source_path).name}\")
            except Exception:
                meta_bits.append(f\"Draw source: {source_path}\")
        if draws_used:
            meta_bits.append(f\"Draws used: {draws_used}\")
        if meta_bits:
            st.caption(\" | \".join(meta_bits))


    def _render_vtrac_box(payload):
        data = payload.get(\"data\", {})
        vstat = data.get(\"vstat\") or {}
        if not vstat:
            st.caption(\"No V-TRAC data\")
            return
        pair_status = data.get(\"pair_status\") or {}
        draws = data.get(\"draws\") or []
        draws_1000 = data.get(\"draws_1000\") or draws
        sums_stats = data.get(\"sums_stats\") or {}
        index_first_seen = {}
        total_len = len(draws_1000)
        try:
            for idx, draw_value in enumerate(draws_1000):
                if not isinstance(draw_value, str) or len(draw_value) "'!= 3 or len(set(draw_value)) == 1:
                    continue
                idx_tmp = get_vtrac_index(draw_value)
                if idx_tmp and idx_tmp not in index_first_seen:
                    index_first_seen[idx_tmp] = idx
        except Exception:
            index_first_seen = {}
        index_draws_since_overlay = {i: index_first_seen.get(i, total_len) for i in range(1, 36)}
        sorted_overlay = sorted(index_draws_since_overlay.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1], reverse=True)
        top10_overlay = [i for i, _ds in sorted_overlay[:10]]
        rows = []
        for entry in VTRAC_DISPLAY:
            idx = entry["Index"]
            singles = entry.get("Singles", "").split()
            doubles = entry.get("Doubles", "").split()
            sdict = vstat.get(idx, {}).get("singles_status", {})
            ddict = vstat.get(idx, {}).get("doubles_status", {})
            s_html = " ".join([
                (_format_combo(combo, sdict, pair_status) + _sums_badge_for(combo, sums_stats))
                for combo in singles
            ]) if singles else "&nbsp;"
            d_html = " ".join([
                (_format_combo(combo, ddict, pair_status) + _sums_badge_for(combo, sums_stats))
                for combo in doubles
            ]) if doubles else "&nbsp;"
            idx_style = vstat.get(idx, {}).get("index_style", {})
            row_class = ""
            badge_html = ""
            if idx_style.get("bg") == "green":
                row_class = "row-green"
                rank_value = idx_style.get("rank")
                badge_html = f"<sup class='"'rank-badge'>{rank_value}</sup>\" if rank_value else \"\"
            else:
                if idx in top10_overlay:
                    row_class = \"row-red\"
                    rank_num = top10_overlay.index(idx) + 1
                    ds_disp = index_draws_since_overlay.get(idx, \"\")
                    badge_html = f\"<sup class='rank-badge'>{rank_num} ({ds_disp})</sup>\"
                elif idx_style.get(\"bg\") == \"red\":
                    row_class = \"row-red\"
                    rank_value = idx_style.get(\"rank\")
                    badge_html = f\"<sup class='rank-badge'>{rank_value}</sup>\" if rank_value else \"\"
            def wrap_cell(content):
                return f\"<div class='{row_class}'>{content}</div>\" if row_class else content
            index_cell = wrap_cell(f\"{idx}{badge_html}\")
            singles_cell = wrap_cell(f\"{s_html}{badge_html}\")
            doubles_cell = wrap_cell(f\"{d_html}{badge_html}\")
            rows.append({\"Index\": index_cell, \"Singles\": singles_cell, \"Doubles\": doubles_cell})
        if rows:
            df_v = pd.DataFrame(rows)
            st.markdown(df_v.to_html(escape=False, index=False), unsafe_allow_html=True)
        else:
            st.caption(\"V-TRAC table unavailable\")


    def _render_overdue_pairs(payload):
        data = payload.get(\"data\", {})
        nonrep = data.get(\"nonrep\") or {}
        rep = data.get(\"rep\") or {}
        thr_r_red = 107
        thr_r_blue = 71
        thr_nr_red = 56
        thr_nr_blue = 37
        thr_pending = 25
        rep_red = \", \".join(sorted(p for p, ds in rep.items() if ds >= thr_r_red)) or \"None\"
        rep_blue = \", \".join(sorted(p for p, ds in rep.items() if thr_r_blue <= ds < thr_r_red)) or \"None\"
        rep_purple = \", \".join(sorted(p for p, ds in rep.items() if thr_pending <= ds < thr_r_blue)) or \"None\"
        nr_red = \", \".join(sorted(p for p, ds in nonrep.items() if ds >= thr_nr_red)) or \"None\"
        nr_blue = \", \".join(sorted(p for p, ds in nonrep.items() if thr_nr_blue <= ds < thr_nr_red)) or \"None\"
        nr_purple = \", \".join(sorted(p for p, ds in nonrep.items() if thr_pending <= ds < thr_nr_blue)) or \"None\"
        st.markdown(\"**Overdue Pairs**\")
        st.markdown(f\"- **Repeating** — RED: {rep_red}<br>- BLUE: {rep_blue}<br>- PURPLE: {rep_purple}\",
        unsafe_allow_html=True)
        st.markdown(f\"- **Non-Repeating** — RED: {nr_red}<br>- BLUE: {nr_blue}<br>- PURPLE: {nr_purple}\",
        unsafe_allow_html=True)


    def _render_four_panels(payload):
        data = payload.get(\"data\", {})
        draws = data.get(\"draws\") or []
        pair_status = data.get(\"pair_status\") or {}
        vstat = data.get(\"vstat\") or {}
        sums_stats = data.get(\"sums_stats\") or {}
        combos_stats = vstat.get(0, {}) if isinstance(vstat, dict) else {}
        singles_ds = combos_stats.get(\"singles_ds\", {}) if isinstance(combos_stats, dict) else {}
        doubles_ds = combos_stats.get(\"doubles_ds\", {}) if isinstance(combos_stats, dict) else {}
        st.markdown(\"**Latest Draws**\")
        if draws:
            st.dataframe(pd.DataFrame({\"Draw\": draws[:5]}), use_container_width=True, hide_index=True)
        else:
            st.caption(\"No draws\")
        st.markdown(\"**Pairs Analysis Results**\")
        times_drawn = {}
        for d in draws[:150]:
            if not isinstance(d, str) or len(d) "'!= 3:
                continue
            d1, d2, d3 = d[0], d[1], d[2]
            for raw in (d1 + d2, d2 + d3, d1 + d3):
                p = "".join(sorted(raw))
                times_drawn[p] = times_drawn.get(p, 0) + 1
        all_pairs = sorted(set(times_drawn.keys()) | set(pair_status.keys()))
        if all_pairs:
            pair_rows = []
            for p in all_pairs:
                overdue_val = pair_status.get(p)
                if isinstance(overdue_val, dict):
                    overdue = overdue_val.get("ds", 0)
                else:
                    overdue = overdue_val or 0
                pair_rows.append({"Pair": p, "Times Drawn": times_drawn.get(p, 0), "Draws Since": overdue})
            st.dataframe(pd.DataFrame(pair_rows).sort_values("Draws Since", ascending=False), use_container_width=True,
        hide_index=True)
        else:
            st.caption("No pair data")
        st.markdown("**Combinations Analysis (Draws Since)**")
        safe_rows = []
        for combo_map, combo_type in ((singles_ds, "Single"), (doubles_ds, "Double")):
            for base, ds in combo_map.items():
                status = {}
                if combo_type == "Single":
                    if ds >= 501:
                        status[base] = {"shape_red_circle": True}
                    elif ds >= 334:
                        status[base] = {"shape_blue_square": True}
                else:
                    if ds >= 1000:
                        status[base] = {"shape_red_circle": True}
                    elif ds >= 667:
                        status[base] = {"shape_blue_square": True}
                html_combo = _format_combo(str(base).zfill(3), status, pair_status)
                safe_rows.append({"Combo": html_combo, "Type": combo_type, "Draws Since": int(ds)})
        if safe_rows:
            dfc_html = pd.DataFrame(sorted(safe_rows, key=lambda x: x["Draws Since"], reverse=True))
            st.markdown("<div style='"'max-height: 280px; overflow-y: auto;'>\" + dfc_html.to_html(escape=False,
        index=False) + \"</div>\", unsafe_allow_html=True)
        else:
            st.caption(\"No combination data\")
        if callable(_build_sums_df) and isinstance(sums_stats, dict) and sums_stats.get(\"by_sum\"):
            try:
                df_sums = _build_sums_df(sums_stats)
                st.markdown(\"**Sums Tracking**\")
                st.markdown(\"<div style='max-height: 280px; overflow-y: auto;'>\" + df_sums.to_html(escape=False,
        index=False) + \"</div>\", unsafe_allow_html=True)
            except Exception:
                st.caption(\"Sums tracking unavailable\")


    def _render_vtrac_index_hits(payload):
        data = payload.get(\"data\", {})
        vstat = data.get(\"vstat\") or {}
        draws = data.get(\"draws\") or []
        draws_1000 = data.get(\"draws_1000\") or draws
        index_first_seen = {}
        total_len = len(draws_1000)
        for i, d in enumerate(draws_1000):
            if not isinstance(d, str) or len(d) "'!= 3 or len(set(d)) == 1:
                continue
            idx = get_vtrac_index(d)
            if idx and idx not in index_first_seen:
                index_first_seen[idx] = i
        index_draws_since = {i: index_first_seen.get(i, total_len) for i in range(1, 36)}
        sorted_ds = sorted(index_draws_since.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1], reverse=True)
        top10_overdue = [i for i, _ in sorted_ds[:10]]
        rows = []
        for idx, ds in index_draws_since.items():
            style = vstat.get(idx, {}).get("index_style", {}) if isinstance(vstat, dict) else {}
            status_label = "Tracking"
            if idx in top10_overdue:
                status_label = "Overdue"
            elif style.get("bg") == "green":
                status_label = "Recent"
            rows.append({"Index": idx, "Draws Since": ds, "Status": status_label})
        if rows:
            df_idx = pd.DataFrame(rows)
            st.markdown("**V-TRAC Index Hits**")
            st.dataframe(df_idx.sort_values(["Status", "Draws Since"], ascending=[True, False]),
        use_container_width=True, hide_index=True)
        else:
            st.caption("No V-TRAC index data")

                        top5 = get_top_overdue_repeating_pairs(draws_window, 5)
                    except Exception:
                        top5 = []

                draws_since_double = 0

                    try:
                        doubles = get_doubles_history({state_name: draws})
                        draws_since_double = doubles.get(state_name, 0)
                    except Exception:
                        doubles = {}
                        draws_since_double = 0

                return {
                    \"variant\": variant,
                    \"source\": source,
                    \"draws\": draws,
                    \"draws_window\": draws_window,
                    \"draws_1000\": draws_1000,
                    \"analysis_window\": analysis_window,
                    \"nonrep\": nonrep,
                    \"rep\": rep,
                    \"pair_status\": pair_status,
                    \"vstat\": vstat,
                    \"top5\": top5,
                    \"doubles\": doubles,
                    \"draws_since_double\": draws_since_double,
                }
