        def _tracker_cell_html(cell) -> str:
            digit_style = \"background-color:#ffea70;padding:0 6px;display:inline-block;font-weight:600;\"
            if getattr(cell, \"hard_due\", False):
                digit_style += \"color:#d60000;\"
            return f\"<span style='{digit_style}'>{cell.digit}</span>\"

        def _render_tracker_grid(variant_result, source_path: str | None) -> None:
            if not variant_result or not variant_result.draws_used:
                st.caption(\"No data\")
                return
            if source_path:
                try:
                    src_name = Path(source_path).name
                except Exception:
                    src_name = source_path
                st.caption(f\"Draw source: {src_name} ({variant_result.draws_used})\")
            grid = variant_result.tracker_grid
            max_rows = max((len(grid.get(pos, [])) for pos in (0, 1, 2)), default=0)
            if max_rows == 0:
                st.caption(\"No positional ranks available\")
                return
            rows = []
            for ridx in range(max_rows):
                row = {\"Rank\": ridx + 1}
                for pos in (0, 1, 2):
                    cells = grid.get(pos, [])
                    if ridx < len(cells):
                        cell = cells[ridx]
                        row[f\"P{pos + 1} DS\"] = cell.draws_since
                        row[f\"P{pos + 1} DIG\"] = _tracker_cell_html(cell)
                    else:
                        row[f\"P{pos + 1} DS\"] = \"\"
                        row[f\"P{pos + 1} DIG\"] = \"\"
                rows.append(row)
            df = pd.DataFrame(rows)
            st.markdown(df.to_html(escape=False, index=False), unsafe_allow_html=True)

        def _render_consensus_summary(report) -> None:
            if not report:
                return
            st.markdown(\"**Cross-variant pressure summary**\")
            summary_cols = st.columns(3)
            for pos_idx, column in enumerate(summary_cols):
                with column:
                    st.markdown(f\"P{pos_idx + 1}\")
                    agg_items = report.aggregated_digits.get(pos_idx, [])
                    if not agg_items:
                        st.caption(\"--\")
                        continue
                    rows = []
                    for agg in agg_items[:5]:
                        hits = \", \".join(
                            f\"{variant[:1].upper()}#{rank}\" for variant, rank in agg.occurrences
                        )
                        rows.append(
                            {
                                \"Digit\": agg.digit,
                                \"Score\": round(agg.score, 2),
                                \"Hits\": hits,
                                \"Tags\": \" \".join(sorted(agg.tags)),
                            }
                        )
                    st.dataframe(pd.DataFrame(rows), use_container_width=True, hide_index=True)
            note_set = sorted({note for note in report.consensus_notes if note})
            if note_set:
                st.caption(\"; \".join(note_set))
            if report.candidates:
                st.markdown(\"**Positional shortlist**\")
                candidate_rows = []
                for cand in report.candidates:
                    candidate_rows.append(
                        {
                            \"Combo\": cand.combo,
                            \"Score\": round(cand.score, 2),
                            \"Ranks\": \"-\".join(str(r) for r in cand.ranks if r),
                            \"Root\": cand.digital_root,
                            \"VTRAC\": \"\" if cand.vtrac_index is None else cand.vtrac_index,
                            \"Tags\": \" \".join(sorted(cand.tags)),
                        }
                    )
                st.dataframe(pd.DataFrame(candidate_rows), use_container_width=True, hide_index=True)

        def _render_pairs_section(variant_meta):
            st.subheader(\"Overdue Pairs Tracker (window = 360 draws)\")
            cols = st.columns(len(variant_meta))
            for col, meta in zip(cols, variant_meta):

                    nonrep = result.get(\"nonrep\", {})
                    if rep:
                        st.markdown(\"<b>Repeating pairs</b>\", unsafe_allow_html=True)
                        for pair, gap in sorted(rep.items(), key=lambda kv: kv[1], reverse=True)[:12]:

                            label = f\"{pair} ({gap})\"
                            if cls:
                                st.markdown(f\"<span class='{cls}'>{label}</span>\", unsafe_allow_html=True)
                            else:
                                st.write(label)
                    else:
                        st.caption(\"No repeating pairs in window\")
                    if nonrep:

    lines.append('                if ridx < len(cells):')
    lines.append('                    cell = cells[ridx]')

• I’m reviewing the root cause: the loader’s _load_project_module doesn’t insert the module into sys.modules, causing
  dataclasses to fail on import. The plan is to update this loader function—specifically at src/app.py:82—to register
  the module before executing it. I’ll outline reproducing the error, explaining the aliasing causing the problem,
  and propose confirming the fix by rerunning smoke tests and verifying UI updates after successful module load.
  I’ll also consider the impact on other modules and mention relevant file references like the dataclass at modules/
  module_d_auxiliary_tools/refactored/positional_tool.py:32. This should give the user a clear troubleshooting summary
  and solid next steps.

> What I found

  - Reproduced the same “Positional module unavailable: 'NoneType' object has no attribute 'dict'” message by calling
  _load_positional_tool_real() directly (src/app.py:82). The loader tries to execute modules/module_d_auxiliary_tools/
  refactored/positional_tool.py, but the dataclass decorator fails inside Python’s dataclasses module because the module
  isn’t registered in sys.modules under the loader’s alias (project_positional_tool). That’s why the AttributeError
  mentions .dict—dataclasses looks up sys.modules[cls.__module__] and gets None.
  - A normal import (import modules.module_d_auxiliary_tools.refactored.positional_tool) works fine; after that,
  analyze_state_variants runs cleanly and produces the expected tracker grid (I spot‑checked with Connecticut). So the
  engine itself is healthy; only the bespoke loader is breaking the import.

  Why it broke

  - Our new positional module defines multiple @dataclass classes (e.g. PositionalTrackerCell at modules/
  module_d_auxiliary_tools/refactored/positional_tool.py:32). The helper _load_project_module fabricates a module name
  (project_positional_tool) but never adds it to sys.modules, so the dataclass decorator can’t resolve the module when
  it inspects cls.__module__.

  Proposed fix (next steps)

  1. Update _load_project_module (src/app.py:82) to register the module before spec.loader.exec_module(mod), e.g.
  sys.modules[dotted_name] = mod. Alternatively, load the positional tool with its real dotted path so dataclasses sees
  the canonical entry—either approach prevents the NoneType.__dict__ failure.
  2. Re-run the sanity checks (python -m py_compile src/app.py, python scripts/checks/smoke_positional.py) to confirm
  the loader now works even when Streamlit cold-imports it.
  3. Once the loader succeeds, the in-app expander will execute the new column layout and the tracker grid should

> I dug into the Aux page mojibake and the duplicate Top‑5 panel. The garbled text comes from UTF‑8 strings that were
  saved through a Windows‑1252 lens—nearly every literal in the Aux block (headings, captions, legends, download labels)
  carries those mis-encoded byte sequences. The duplicate “Top 5 Most Overdue Repeating Pairs” block is the older panel
  that still uses the wrong color logic (red when ≥ 107, blue when ≥ 71); the later block inside the four-column layout
  is already correct.

  Because the mojibake spans many literal strings in src/app.py, a safe fix needs to do three things in one pass:
  list.

  I started implementing a scripted replacement, but it became clear the garbled bytes are scattered across multiple
  regions; a proper solution means rewriting the affected panel and captions rather than spot-replacing substrings.
  Before I proceed further (and risk missing a spot), I wanted to confirm the expectations:

  - Should all text be plain ASCII (no emoji/bullets), e.g., “V-TRAC Index Hits (Working logic)”, “Red (>= 71)”?

            rows_pairs.append({"Pair": pair, "Times Drawn": times_drawn.get(pair, 0), "Draws Since": overdue})
        df_pairs = _pd.DataFrame(rows_pairs)
        if not df_pairs.empty:
            df_pairs = df_pairs.sort_values("Draws Since", ascending=False)
        st.dataframe(df_pairs, use_container_width=True)
    with combos_col:
        st.subheader("Combinations Analysis (Draws Since)")
        combo_ds = vstat.get(0, {})
        singles_ds = combo_ds.get("singles_ds", {})
        doubles_ds = combo_ds.get("doubles_ds", {})
        safe_rows = []
        for base, ds in singles_ds.items():
            status = {}
            if ds >= THR_COMB_RED_SINGLE:
                status[base] = {"shape_red_circle": True}
            elif ds >= THR_COMB_BLUE_SINGLE:
                status[base] = {"shape_blue_square": True}
            html_combo = _format_combo(str(base).zfill(3), status, pair_status)
            safe_rows.append({"Combo": html_combo, "Type": "Single", "Draws Since": int(ds)})
        for base, ds in doubles_ds.items():
            status = {}
            if ds >= THR_COMB_RED_DOUBLE:
                status[base] = {"shape_red_circle": True}
            elif ds >= THR_COMB_BLUE_DOUBLE:
                status[base] = {"shape_blue_square": True}
            html_combo = _format_combo(str(base).zfill(3), status, pair_status)
            safe_rows.append({"Combo": html_combo, "Type": "Double", "Draws Since": int(ds)})
        if safe_rows:
            safe_rows.sort(key=lambda row: row["Draws Since"], reverse=True)
            dfc_html = _pd.DataFrame(safe_rows)
            html_table = dfc_html.to_html(escape=False, index=False)
            st.markdown(
                f'"'<div style=\"max-height: 420px; overflow-y: auto; border: 1px solid #eee; padding: 6px;
        \">{html_table}</div>',
                unsafe_allow_html=True,
            )
        else:
            st.write(\"No data\")
    with top5_col:
        st.subheader(\"Top 5 Most Overdue Repeating Pairs (Working logic)\")
        for pair, overdue in results.get(\"top5\", []):
            if overdue >= THR_R_BLUE:
                color = \"red\"
            elif overdue >= THR_R_RED:
                color = \"blue\"
            elif overdue >= THR_PENDING:
                color = \"purple\"
            else:
                color = \"\"
            line = f\"{pair} - {overdue} draws overdue\"
            else:
                st.write(line)
    if callable(_build_sums_df) and isinstance(sums_stats, dict) and sums_stats.get(\"by_sum\"):

• Ran
  └ powershell.exe -NoProfile -Command '$code = @'"'
    from pathlib import Path

    path = Path('src/app.py')
    text = path.read_text(encoding='utf-8')

    def double_fix(s: str) -> str:
        return s.encode('cp1252', errors='ignore').decode('utf-8', errors='ignore')

    fixed = double_fix(double_fix(text))

    def seq(bytes_list):
        return bytes(bytes_list).decode('cp1252', errors='ignore')

    seq_map = {
        seq([0xE2,0x80,0xA2]): '&#8226;',  # â€¢
        seq([0xE2,0x89,0xA5]): '>=',       # â‰¥
        seq([0xE2,0xA6,0xBF]): '|',        # â¦¿
        seq([0xE2,0xAC,0x87]): '',         # â¬‡
        seq([0xE2,0x80,0xA6]): '...',      # â€¦
        seq([0xE2,0x80,0x93]): '-',       # â€“
        seq([0xE2,0x80,0x94]): '-',       # â€”
        seq([0xE2,0x80,0x9C]): '\"',       # â€œ
        seq([0xE2,0x80,0x9D]): '\"',       # â€
        seq([0xE2,0x80,0x99]): \"'\",      # â€™
        seq([0xE2,0x80,0x98]): \"'\",      # â€˜
        seq([0xE2,0x9C,0x85]): '',        # âœ…
        seq([0xE2,0x80,0xA1]): '',        # â€¡
        seq([0xE2,0x80,0xA0]): '',        # â€
        seq([0xE2,0x80,0xB0]): ' per ',   # â€°
        seq([0xE2,0x86,0x92]): '->',      # â†’
        seq([0xE2,0x80,0xA1]): '',        # repetition ok
        seq([0xE2,0x80,0xBA]): '>',       # â€º maybe
        seq([0xCE,0xA3]): 'Sigma',        # Σ
        seq([0xC5,0x93]): 'oe',           # œ
        seq([0xC5,0xA1]): 's',            # š
        seq([0xC5,0xB8]): 'Y',            # Ÿ
        seq([0xE2,0x82,0xAC]): 'EUR',     # €
        seq([0xC2,0xBC]): '1/4',          # ¼
        seq([0xC2,0xA2]): 'c',            # ¢
        seq([0xC2,0xA5]): 'Y',            # ¥
        seq([0xC2,0xA6]): '|',            # ¦
        seq([0xC2,0xAC]): '-',            # ¬
        seq([0xC2,0xB6]): '',             # ¶
        seq([0xC2,0xBF]): '?',            # ¿
        seq([0xF0,0x9F,0x9A,0x80]): ':rocket:', # ðŸš€
    }

    # Specific word replacements
    fixed = fixed.replace(seq([0x56,0xE2,0x80,0x91]), 'V-')  # Vâ€‘
    fixed = fixed.replace('State ' + seq([0xE2,0x80,0x93,0xBC]), 'State')
